  Pistachio Crusted Chicken Breasts with Sun-Dried
                         Cherry and Orange Sauce
Ingredients                        Directions
2 cups chopped pistachio nuts     Preheat the oven to 325 degrees F (165 degrees C). Mix together
1 cup panko bread crumbs          the pistachios and bread crumbs in a bowl. Beat the egg whites in a
2 egg whites                      separate bowl, and set aside.
4 skinless, boneless chicken
breast halves                     Season the chicken breasts with salt and pepper, gently dip them
salt and black pepper to taste    into the beaten egg whites, and then press into the pistachio-bread
1 tablespoon vegetable oil        crumb mixture to coat. Gently toss between your hands so any
                                  coating that hasn't stuck can fall away.
2 tablespoons butter
                                  Heat the oil and butter in an oven-safe skillet over medium heat.
Sauce:                            Gently place the chicken breasts into the hot skillet, and fry for 5 to
1 teaspoon butter                 8 minutes, until the bottom coating is golden brown and crisp.
2 shallots, finely chopped
1/2 cup red wine                  Turn the chicken over in the skillet, and place the skillet in the
4 ounces dried cherries           preheated oven. Bake for about 30 minutes, until the chicken is no
1 cup freshly squeezed orange     longer pink, the juices run clear, and the top coating is lightly
juice                             browned.
1 1/2 cups chicken stock
1 teaspoon grated orange zest     While the chicken is baking, melt 1 teaspoon of butter in a
                                  saucepan over medium-low heat, add the shallots, and cook for 2
                                  to 3 minutes until tender. Pour in the wine, drop in the cherries, and
                                  cook and stir for about 10 minutes to reduce wine to a glaze. Pour
                                  in the orange juice, chicken stock, and orange zest, and cook and
                                  stir about 20 more minutes, until sauce is reduced and thickened.
